## Document 002: 10月FOMC議事要旨：12月利上げ視野、少数ながら50bp議論 ～FRBは2027年のインフレ鈍化シナリオを維持～

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月FOMC議事要旨：12月利上げ視野、少数ながら50bp議論   ～FRBは2027年のインフレ鈍化シナリオを維持～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

FED  Watching

2026 年 11 月 19 日

1/4 
10 月 FOMC 議事要旨：12 月利上げ視野、少数ながら 50bp 議論 
～FRB は 2027 年のインフレ鈍化シナリオを維持～ 
 経済調査部 主任エコノミスト  桂畑 誠治  
（℡：050-5474-7493）

要旨  
・12 月利上げ再開がメインシナリオ：10 月 27〜28 日開催のFOMC議事要旨が公表された。
同会合では政策金利の据え置きが決定されたものの、参加者のほぼ全員が「今後数カ月間（12 月
15〜16 日会合を含む）での利上げ再開」を基本路線として維持していることが確認された。

・タカ派的テールリスクの浮上：一方で、2〜3 名の参加者がデータ次第で「50bpの大幅利上
げ」を検討する余地に言及しており、市場ではタカ派的なテールリスクが意識される内容となっ
た。

・2027 年のインフレ鈍化見通しは不変：FRBのベースライン（基本見通し）では、インフレは
目先高止まりするものの、関税影響の減退やエネルギー・AI関連の供給制約緩和に伴い、2027
年にかけて鈍化し、時間の経過とともに目標の 2％へ収束していくとの見方は崩れていない。

1．新体制下での情報発信と議事 要旨 の重要性  
新体制移行後、会合直後の声明文は大幅に簡素化され、議長の記者会見も短縮されるなど、直
近の直接的な情報発信は絞り込まれている。その分、後日公表される議事要旨は高い情報量を維
持しており、FRBの本音や委員会内部の議論の分布を推し量る最重要ツールとしての重みが増して
いる。もっとも、公表がFOMC終了から 3 週間後となるため、その間に発表された最新データとの
タイムラグ（情報の遅効性）を考慮して評価する必要がある。

2．10 月会合 の政策 判断 と金融 政策 の基本 スタンス  
10 月会合では、FF レート誘導目標レンジ（3.75％〜4.00％）の据え置きが決定された（投票権
を持つ委員ベースでは 12 名中 10 名が賛成、2 名が反対）。据え置きを支持した委員は、「さらなる
情報の蓄積が、インフレ率を 2％の目標へ適時に戻すための適切な政策スタンスを見極める上で役
立つ」と指摘した。今回の据え置きは利上げサイクルの終了ではなく、累積的な引き締め効果と最
新データを確認するための「一時停止（スキップ）」という位置づけである。

### Page 2

2/4 
今後の政策決定については、「新たに入手されるデータ、変化する見通し、リスクバランスに依存
する」とのデータ次第（データ・ディペンデント）の姿勢を強調した。その上で、基本見通しの下
では「9 月の経済・金利予測（SEP：議長自身は今回も提出せず）とおおむね整合的な形で、今後数
カ月間（12 月 15〜16 日の次回会合など）で追加利上げを行うことが適切となる可能性が高い」と
の見解で、参加者のほぼ全員が一致した。

3．経済・物価・雇用の現状認識と 中長期 の見通し  
委員会全体の共通認識を確認すると、景気については「底堅い家計支出と堅調な設備投資に支え
られ、経済活動は引き続き力強いペースで拡大している」との見方で一致した。 
労働市場については「安定しており、最大雇用に近づいている」と判断され、目先も安定した状
態が続き、雇用の上振れリスクと下振れリスクはおおむね均衡していると評価された。 
インフレに関しては、依然として高止まりしており、物価上昇圧力は経済全体に広範に及んでい
るとの指摘が相次いだ。特に高いエネルギー価格や精製燃料の供給制約が足元の押し上げ要因とな
っており、見通しには上振れリスクが意識されている。一方、名目賃金の伸びは緩やかな水準にと
どまり、2％のインフレ目標とおおむね整合的と判断された。 
中長期のインフレ見通しでは、9 月コア CPI（前月比＋0.3％）の上振れを受けても、スタッフの
インフレ予測は 9 月会合時点からほとんど変更されず、参加者の間でも従来の基本見通しが維持さ
れた。大半の参加者は、インフレ率は目先高止まりするものの、関税引き上げに伴う価格転嫁圧力
の減退や、エネルギー価格・AI 関連投資に伴う供給制約（電力・インフラ需要等）の緩和により、
時間の経過とともに（9 月 SEP で示された 2027 年の鈍化軌道に沿って）2％に向けて低下していく
と予想した。中長期的なインフレ期待も引き続き 2％と整合的であり、これが過度な利上げ回数の
積み増し（ターミナルレートの大幅引き上げ）を抑制する根拠となっている。

4．タカ派サプライズ：「 12 月 50bp 利上げ」への言及と今後の焦点  
今回の議事要旨における最大の注目点（タカ派サプライズ）は、9 月 SEP に沿った追加利上げ（25bp
ずつ）というメインシナリオの裏で、大幅利上げの選択肢が議論されていたことである。「新たに入
手されるインフレデータが上振れれば、次回 12 月会合で 25bp を超える利上げ（50bp など）が検討
に値する」と発言した参加者が 2〜3 名いたことが明らかとなった。 
議事要旨の公表前にあたる11月10日に発表された10月コアCPIは前月比＋0.3％と3カ月連続
で市場予想を上回り、3 カ月年率換算ペースでは目標を大幅に上回る高い伸びとなった。さらに、
10 月会合では据え置きに賛成したウォラーFRB 理事も、10 月 CPI の結果を受けて「50bp の利上げ
を検討すべき」との趣旨の発言を行っており、同理事は議事要旨内の「タカ派の少数意見」に含ま
れていたとみられる。

5．マーケットへのインプリケーション  
現時点では 50bp 利上げは少数意見にとどまるが、FRB が「インフレ再燃リスクに対して後手に回

がある。仮に市場金利の低下などによって金融環境の引き締まりが緩めば、利上げペースの加速に
追い込まれる可能性が高い。
12 月 FOMC に向けては、今後発表される 11 月の雇用統計、CPI、ISM 景気指数の結果次第で、市
場の織り込みが「25bp 利上げ」から「50bp 利上げ」へと急速に傾くリスクがある。債券市場ではタ
ーミナルレートの上振れを意識する形で米 2 年債利回りに上昇圧力がかかりやすく、為替市場でも
ドル高・円安地合いが継続しやすい展開が見込まれる。

## Document 004: 10月企業物価指数 ～前年比＋7.1％、電力や石油・石炭製品を中心に幾分加速～

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月企業物価指数 ～前年比＋7.1％、電力や石油・石炭製品を中心に幾分加速～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-12
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Indicators

2026 年 11 月 12 日

1/2 
10 月企業物価指数 
～前年比＋7.1％、電力や石油・石炭製品を中心に幾分加速～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  阿原 健一郎 
（℡：050-5470-4868）

1. 国内企業物価指数は幾分加速 
 本日公表された 10 月の国内企業物価指数は前年比＋7.1％と、前月（同＋6.8％）から 
伸びが幾分加速した。前年比寄与度で内訳を見ると、玄米や豚肉などの「農林水産物」のマイナ
ス寄与が前月から拡大した一方、「電力・都市ガス・水道」や「石油・石炭製品」、「化学製品」の
プラス寄与が前月から拡大し、ヘッドラインを押し上げた（図表１）。 
 背景には、原油やナフサの価格上昇が石油製品や化学製品へ波及していることに加え、電力に
おいても、過去の輸入燃料価格の上昇がタイムラグを伴って燃料費調整単価に反映され、全体を
押し上げていることがある。農林水産物の価格下落が一部で下押し要因となっているものの、エ
ネルギー関連を中心としたコスト上昇圧力は依然として根強い。

２. 輸入物価指数はわずかに加速 
 10 月の輸入物価指数は前年比＋24.8％と、前月（同＋23.8％）から伸びがわずかに加速した。
「石油・石炭・天然ガス」のプラス寄与が前月から拡大し、ヘッドラインを押し上げた（図表
２）。 
 為替変動の影響を除いた契約通貨ベースでも同＋19.2％と、前月（同＋17.3％）から伸びが拡大
しており、海外の資源価格上昇が輸入物価を押し上げている。輸入コストの増加が時間差を伴っ
て国内の製品価格に波及することを踏まえると、先行きも価格転嫁を通じて消費者物価への上昇
圧力が残る可能性がある。

以 上

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 11 月 16 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 黃仁勳請辭衝擊輝達，AI 需求韌性支撐科技股反彈：輝達執行長黃仁勳因涉及國安疑慮的相
關爭議，11/12 正式辭任執行長及董事職務，由財務長 Colette Kress 暫代，輝達當日股價盤
中一度重挫近 12%，惟隨後跌幅收斂收跌 4.3%。領導層更迭引發市場對公司策略及 AI 產業
發展的疑慮，但 CoreWeave 第三季營收年增 168%，因受惠 AI 算力需求強勁，此一利多顯
示 AI 需求尚未顯著轉弱，惟輝達管理團隊穩定性、後續財報及政策調查進展，仍是影響科技

股評價的重要變數。
◼ 美國 10 月通膨高於預期，Fed 升息預期推升美債殖利率：美國 11/10 公布 10 月 CPI 月增
0.35%，高於預期的 0.3%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%，亦高於預期的 0.2%，顯示通膨壓力仍具黏
性。因 2025 年政府停擺造成資料缺漏，本次未公布年增率。Fed 理事 Waller 表示，若核心
通膨維持 0.3%左右，12 月應考慮升息 2 碼，市場因此提高對後續緊縮的預期。
◼ 伊朗上週末再度對美軍設施進行攻擊，惟美方尚無反擊行動：美國國務卿魯比歐 11/13 表示，
美國對伊朗造成的經濟壓力已大到沒有必要採取軍事行動。不過，伊朗革命衛隊上週末以無人
機攻擊美軍在巴林的雷達站，但美軍尚無進一步反擊，留意之後美方是否仍以封鎖取代攻擊。
市場展望
◼ 科技股短期審慎、中期仍正向，AI 供應鏈基本面持續具支撐：黃仁勳辭任使輝達面臨管理層
接班、企業治理及政策調查等不確定性，加上 Fed 升息預期及 AI 資料中心監管疑慮，市場
短線波動可能延續。然而，該事件並未直接改變 AI 算力需求，且上週 CoreWeave Q3 財報

營收年增 168%、台積電 10 月營收年增 59.7%，均顯示 AI 基礎建設投資仍具動能。整體對
科技股維持短期審慎、但中長期基本面仍在，台灣及日本等具技術競爭優勢、獲利與現金流
穩健的半導體及 AI 供應鏈亦值得留意。惟市場焦點已由資本支出規模轉向投資報酬與現金
流，後續仍須關注輝達財報及管理團隊能否維持客戶信心。
◼ Fed 升息預期壓抑債市，中天期美債及投資級債仍相對具吸引力： 美國 10 月 CPI 高於預
期，加上 Fed 官員偏鷹表態，使債券市場持續承受政策利率上修壓力。美國 10 年期公債殖
利率重回 5%以上，收益水準已具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券雖然價格波動較小，但殖
利率較低，且未來仍面臨政策利率及再投資風險；超長天期債券則易受到財政赤字、發債供
給與期限溢價影響。中天期美債在收益與利率風險之間相對均衡，維持審慎正向看法。信用
債方面，投資級債收益條件仍具支撐，惟須留意高資本支出企業的融資與現金流風險。

2357 華碩電腦(ASUS)

報告日期：2026/11/17 研究員：曾智楷(Kevin Tseng) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 品牌營收 2588 億，QoQ +7%，毛利率 15.9.6%，EPS 
29.10 元。 
 公司預估 4Q26 伺服器營收將季增 100%，由於部分客戶第三
季因建置時程延後，使伺服器出貨與營收遞延至第四季認列。
PC 與零組件業務方面，受到記憶體等零組件價格上漲壓抑終
端市場需求，預估 4Q26 電腦業務營收季減 15%，零組件則預
估將季減 10%。 
 受惠於 GPU AI 伺服器需求暢旺，公司預估 2026 全年伺服器
營收將成長 150%，並 預 估 2027 年伺服器營收可望維持雙位數
至高雙位數的年增率。

一. 受惠伺服器、PC 業務成長，3Q26 EPS 29.1 元 
華碩 3Q26 品牌營收 2587.9 億元，QoQ +7%，YoY +36%，主要
受惠於伺服器、PC 業務成長，帶動第三季營收創歷史新高。就產
品組合來看，3Q26 系統產品(System)佔整體營收的 50%，開放
平台(Open Platform)佔 20%，基礎設施解決方案事業群(IS)佔
30%。公司表示，面對 DRAM 等零組件漲價逆風，第三季 PC 營
收仍年增超過 25% ，主要由於公司高價值產品，包含電競
Gaming、高階 AI PC 及商用 PC，佔 PC 營收比重超過 65%，且
商用 PC 出貨量維持強勁，年成長超過 30%。另外，伺服器業務
方面，儘管部分訂單遞延至第四季認列，第三季伺服器營收仍實
現超過 100%的年增長；通用型 CPU 伺服器亦受惠於企業擴建地
端資料中心以支援 AI 工作負載，營收年增超過 80%。獲利方面，
3Q26 營業毛利 412 億，QoQ +3%，YoY +68%，毛利率 15.9%，
QoQ -0.6ppts，YoY +3.0ppts，營業利益 219.6 億，QoQ +12%，
YoY +161%，營利率 8.5%，QoQ +0.4ppts，YoY +4.1ppts，3Q26
業外利益 35.9 億，稅後淨利 216.3 億，QoQ +14%，YoY +105%，
單季稅後 EPS 29.1 元。

二. 4Q26 伺服器遞延訂單放量抵銷 PC 逆風 
公司公告 10 月營收 1076 億元，MoM +10.7%，YoY +62.2%。今
年累計營收 8500 億，YoY +41.1%。展望 4Q26，受惠於伺服器
呈現強勁成長，公司預估 4Q26 伺服器營收季增 100%，年增
280%，主要由於部分資料中心客戶於第三季因建置時程延後，使
伺服器出貨與營收遞延至第四季認列。而 PC 與零組件業務方面，

受到記憶體等零組件價格上漲推升終端 ASP 影響，壓抑終端市場
需求，公司預估 4Q26 電腦業務營收季減 15%，年增 15%。零組
件則預估將季減 10%，年減 15%。預估公司 4Q26 營收 3106 億
元，QoQ +12.4%，YoY +53.1%，毛利率 14.8%，營利率 6.3%，

三. 預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 89.30/86.35 元 
展望 2026 年，在伺服器業務方面，公司表示目前整體訂單能見度
強勁，除了受惠於 GPU AI 伺服器需求暢旺之外，企業擴建地端資
料中心，亦帶動通用型 CPU 伺服器需求同步提升。在第四季翻倍
季增帶動下，公司預估 2026 全年伺服器營收達成至少 150%的年
成長目標。而 PC 與零組件業務，則受記憶體等零組件價格大幅
上漲影響，在 4Q26 至 1H27 的 PC 市場需求逆風與價格成本混亂
期間，PC 業務的獲利能力與營業利益率將承受一定壓力。預估公
司 2026 全年營收 1.05 兆，YoY +42.5%，毛利率 15.8%，營利率
7.2%，稅後淨利 663 億，稅後 EPS 89.30 元。展望 2027 年，PC
整體市場受零組件價格上漲與需求定價混亂影響，公司預估 2027
年全球 PC 市場整體出貨量將年減 10%至 15%，就季度節奏來看，
上半年跌幅較大，下半年將逐步回升。而伺服器業務方面，管理
層則預估 2027 年伺服器營收可望維持雙位數至高雙位數的年增
率。由於公司客戶涵蓋 Tier 2/3 CSP、NeoCloud、大型科技公司、
AI 研究機構、企業地端資料中心及國家級主權 AI 專案，儘管近期
市場面臨資本市場融資條件趨緊以及美國期中大選後各地資料中
心建置監管趨嚴等逆風，但目前訂單需求並未出現明顯延遲或變
化。預估公司 2027 全年營收 1.26 兆，YoY +19.5%，毛利率
14.5%，營利率 6.6%，稅後淨利 641 億，稅後 EPS 86.35 元。

四. 投資建議為 750~950 元區間操作 
公司 AI 伺服器業務維持強勁成長動能，預期 2027 年將維持雙位
數成長表現，但考量到記憶體缺貨情況延續，可能持續影響公司
PC 業務的營運動能。公司目前股價交易於 2027 EPS 約 9~10 倍
本益比，評價上並未明顯偏低，給予區間操作投資建議，目標價
750-950 元(9-11x 2027 EPS)。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
華碩電腦(ASUS)為全球知名筆電品牌，以自有品牌「ASUS」銷
售全球。主要營業項目為 3C 資訊產品(含電腦系統產品、主機板
及各類板卡及智慧型手機等手持裝置等)之設計、研發及銷售。

二.產業趨勢概況 
1.3Q26 全球 PC 出貨量暴跌逾二成，AI 搶走記憶體成衰退元兇 
受 AI 資料中心擴張排擠記憶體與零組件供給影響，2026 年第三
季全球 PC 市場旺季不旺，根據 IDC，3Q26 PC 單季出貨量降至
6,270 萬台，年減 20.1%。由於 DRAM 與 SSD 價格暴漲逾 4 倍，
在 PC 成本占比由 15%飆升至近 40%，加上處理器與顯示卡等元
件短缺，以及業者於上半年提前拉貨透支需求，導致整體市場面
臨沉重的庫存與成本壓力。就各大品牌來看，第三季普遍陷入雙
位數的衰退，聯想第三季出貨量年減-22.6%、惠普-30.9%、戴爾
-25%、蘋果-11.3%，而華碩則展現出強勁的韌性，第三季出貨量
達 550 萬台，年減 8.6%，為全球前五大 PC 品牌中唯一跌幅控制
在個位數的業者，表現明顯優於其他競爭對手。然而研究機構仍

2. 明年 CSP Capex 預估破 1.5 兆美元，AI 伺服器產值年增逾 9 成 
根據集邦科技(TrendForce)最新研究，受惠於 Google、Amazon、
Meta、Microsoft、Oracle 及中國四大科技巨頭等全球前九大雲端
服務供應商(CSP)擴大投資 AI 基礎設施，預估 2026 年九大 CSP
資本支出總額將逾 9,570 億美元，YoY +106%，2027 年更因伺服
器整機漲價、零組件缺貨漲價及供應商要求預付款等因素，有望
突破 1.5 兆美元，YoY +60%，雙雙優於預期。在超大型 CSP、Tier-2
資料中心與主權 AI 等需求帶動下，預估 2026 年 AI 伺服器產值約
4,790 億美元，占整體伺服器產值近 75%；2027 年隨輝達 GB300、
VR200 與超微 Helios 等整櫃式方案成為主流，加上記憶體等關鍵
零組件短缺推升單價，預計整體伺服器產值將首度跨越 1 兆美元，
其中 AI 伺服器產值將躍升至近 9,200 億美元 YoY +90%，占比進
一步提高至 81%左右。

3. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型(LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI 
server 出貨年成長幅度，從原先的 28%更新為近 31%。北美五大
業者 2026 年的資本支出多翻倍提升，反映出大型 CSP 廠持續擴
建 AI 資料中心、GPU 叢集、液冷散熱等新一代基礎設施，以應對
生成式 AI 及大型模型快速成長帶來的算力需求。其中，Google 
2026 至 2027 年都將側重自家 TPU 晶片發展，2027 年出貨量有
望繼續高速成長。AWS 2026 年以 NVIDIA GB300 作為 GPU AI 
server 主力，自研 ASIC 也穩定出貨，預期至 2027 年量能將再擴
大。Meta 2026 年主要採用 NVIDIA GB/VR、AMD Helios 整櫃式
方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，為該公司長期
降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

### Page 1

ab 23 November 2026
Global Research
Initiation of Coverage
Taiwan Union Technology Corporation
Building streams of growth; Initiate at Buy
Initiate on TW CCL                      - TUC: Attractive Buy on business transition
We initiate TUC with a Buy rating and PT of NT$1,150. The company is one of the
leading suppliers for high-speed low-loss CCL materials with increasing presence more
notably in networking switches, servers, power supply units, and satellites, etc. We

believe these new growth areas will drive 65% revenue CAGR through 2026-2028E;
and anticipate product mix shift toward advanced M7+ materials will drive margin
expansion. We see attractive risk-reward with the stock trading at 6x/4x '27/'28E
consensus PE, vs. historical range of 7-17x, backed by   accelerated earnings expansion.
Enhanced profitability as product mix shifts toward M7/M8+
TUC has relatively less high-end CCL exposure compared to EMC (M7+ was 36% of

2025 sales, vs. 50-55% for EMC) but has a stronger position in high-end networking
switch market with potentially growing exposure in AI ASIC projects. We assume  M7+
CCL product sales contribution to reach 65% by 2030E driven by 1) its expanding
footprint in networking, which is upgrading toward M7/M8+ from 800G/1.6T+; 2)
potential traction in ASIC platforms; 3) power supply related sales contribution  to grow
from 5-10% in H225 to 15%+ in 2027E; and 4) potential opportunities in LEO satellites.

We see differentiated growth engines to drive product mix improvement and margin
expansion. We expect EPS to grow at 89% CAGR through 2026-2028E, outpacing
topline growth of 65% for the same period.
Capacity additions to capture  growth with continued pricing adjustments
Targeting opportunities in the material upgrade cycle fuelled by stronger computing/
networking demand and hence also higher power requirement, TUC is investing for
future growth with capacity expansion planned for Thailand and China. Potential new

CCL entrants currently do not have access to equipment and existing suppliers' capacity
expansion is also constrained by extended equipment lead time (now 2 years+) so overall
industry supply shall remain relatively stable through 2028E. Mid to low-end CCL  pricing
uplifts are expected to increase by at least double digits into H226 with upward trend
sustaining into H127 before new e-glass capacity scales up. M7+ high-end CCL have
started to raise pricing in Q426 to reflect raw material increases, and CCL makers expect

potential further pricing uplifts into 2027E as high-end copper foil and low-DK glass
cloth supply might tighten.
Valuation: Initiate at Buy with PT$1,150
In light of market concerns on AI slowdown, we noted that our estimates are below
consensus factoring in slower capacity ramp (4.6mn/5.2mn in '28/'30E, 10-30% lower
than announced plans) and much less pricing uplifts. Our NT$1,150 PT is based on 8x
'28E PE, which we think is fair at the low-end of historical trading range of 7-17x, and

deem conservative with 36% '26-'30E earnings CAGR, implying only 0.2x PEG.  Despite
more conservatives estimates, we still see good upside on current valuation.

Taiwan Union Technology Corporation UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Is favourable pricing environment and current CCL industry upcycle sustainable?
We continue to see AI CCL content value increases as computing and networking/interconnect
demands surges. Extended equipment lead time and qualification process along with increasing
manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more constrained upstream inputs. We expect

persisting CCL industry shortage to drive better pricing power and believe high-end CCL have
entered a spec-driven structural upgrade cycle, not a temporary commodity uptick.
Q: Could high-end CCL drive the next phase of growth for TUC?
While TUC has relatively less high-end CCL revenue exposure than EMC, we still see multiple drivers
lifting its M7+ product exposure that should lead to better growth visibility and margin profile,
including 1) its expanding footprint in networking, which is upgrading toward M7/M8+ from

800G/1.6T+; 2) potential traction in ASIC platforms; 3)  potential opportunities in LEO satellites. We
expect product mix transition to drive accelerated earnings growth in the next few years.
Among CCL supply chain, we believe TUC is looking to benefit from the structural spec upgrades
cycle, with growing presence in AI server,  not aggressively head-to-head in the hot zones, but actually
seeking growth in a number of diversified areas including networking/switch, power supply, and LEO

satellites, etc. We still see multiple drivers lifting its M7+ product exposure that should lead to better
growth visibility and margin profile.
Top 6 CSP capex spending has revised up another leg, strengthening visibility into AI-related CCL
demand. Hyperscaler capex grew 25% QoQ/ 101% YoY in Q326 to US$252.7bn, with UBS 2026/27E
forecasts revised up 2%/18% to imply +96%/+74% YoY growth to US$897bn/US$1.6tn.
Meanwhile, the pricing backdrop remains supportive,   with Taiwanese CCL suppliers raising prices by

10-30% QoQ in Q126 and Q226, followed by further double-digit increases in August and
September. EMC’s latest commentary reinforces this trend,  noting 5-20% price increases across its
portfolio in Q426F and further upside possible should the supply-demand mismatch persist.
Following its sharp re-rating, TUC has corrected 65% from its recent October peak, versus a 28%
decline in the TAIEX, amid broader tech-sector weakness and profit-taking. The shares trade at 6x/4x

2027E/2028E consensus P/E, with the 6x 2027E multiple at one standard deviation below their
historical 12-month forward average of 12x, offering an attractive entry point. We believe the market
underappreciates TUC’s product-mix and profitability improvement, driven by next-generation CSP
penetration, a richer M8/M9 mix and expanding networking, optical and power exposure, supported
by capacity expansion through 2028. We value TUC at 8x 2028E P/E, supported by 36% 2026–30E

earnings CAGR, implying a 0.2x PEG ratio.

Taiwan Union Technology (TUC) was founded in 1974, and is a leading global supplier of copper-clad
laminate (CCL), prepreg and mass-lamination services for printed circuit boards. The company

specializes in high-speed, low-loss materials, with products used across telecom infrastructure, high-
speed networking, servers and data centers, automotive electronics and LEO satellite applications. In
2025, ELL/SLL (M7/M8) accounted for 36% of CCL revenue, VLL/LL (M4/M6) 32%, HDI/N-LL 24%
and other products 8%. TUC’s manufacturing footprint spans Taiwan, Mainland China and Thailand.

Company Description

### Page 3

Taiwan Union Technology Corporation UBS Research
Business overview
Founded in 1974 as Taiwan Union Glass Industrial Co., Ltd., TUC initially manufactured
optical glass before pivoting into CCL and prepreg in 1997 and adding mass-lamination
services in 2001.  The company subsequently built its expertise around high-reliability,
low-loss materials, introducing low-loss high-frequency communication laminates in
2008-09 and second-generation very-low-loss products in 2011-15, which established

its presence in 5G base stations and high-end networking equipment.
As network speeds migrated from 100G/400G toward 800G and AI infrastructure, TUC
progressively upgraded its product portfolio from VLL/LL (M4/M6) to higher-end ELL/SLL
(M7/M8) materials for switches, AI servers and CSP platforms, while also extending into
emerging applications such as power, LEO satellites and optical. According to Prismark,
TUC held 16.4% of the global halogen-free high-speed laminate market in 2025,

ranking No. 3 globally. ELL/SLL and VLL/LL products together accounted for 68% of
2025 CCL revenue versus 24% for HDI/N-LL, reflecting its increasing exposure to high-
speed infrastructure applications.

Networking remains a key structural growth driver with 800G/1.6T share gains
TUC was an early supplier to hyperscale networking applications, with one CSP
customer adopting its materials for 100G data-center switches in 2015, followed by two

other major CSPs in 2016. This established networking franchise is becoming
increasingly valuable as switch speeds advance. Having been underrepresented in the
400G generation, TUC is now gaining share in 800G, supported by its relationships with
leading customers in networking/ethernet/switch/router as well as growing penetration
into white-box switch programs. The transition from 400G to 800G and ultimately 1.6T
should continue to drive both share gains and content growth. While 400G primarily

uses M7-class materials, 800G increasingly requires higher-value M8 solutions and 1.6T
uses M8+ solutions alongside greater PCB complexity and rising optical content.
Management noted that recent demand has exceeded the available capacity of several
Korean and Japanese competitors, creating opportunities for TUC to capture
incremental business from customers planning 2027-28 deployments. The company is
also expanding beyond 400G programs into 800G and early 1.6T platforms. Given

networking's superior margins and customers' greater willingness to pay for
performance and secured capacity, we believe TUC is willing to allocate capacity to
800G/1.6T networking products over AI server products.
AI server penetration expands from ODMs to CSP ASICs
TUC initially built its AI server exposure through ODM-led platforms with a focus on
HGX-related boards,  where increasingly open supplier sourcing has supported share
gains. Its opportunity is also expanding as AI server designs migrate from predominantly

M7 toward M8 qualification, raising the value of TUC’s high-speed CCL offering.

### Page 4

Importantly, management views ODM business as particularly attractive given better
pricing and greater influence over supplier selection, and continues to target HGX
opportunities through these customers.
TUC further broadened its exposure into CSP ASIC platforms in 2025 through a major
ASIC customer's project, with penetration continuing on that next-gen project taking
place in 2026. While this represents an important expansion beyond its traditional ODM

base, management noted this ASIC project pricing is less favorable than ODM business,
suggesting the strategic value lies more in expanding TUC’s presence in large-scale ASIC
platforms than in margin uplift. We expect AI server growth to increasingly come from a
combination of deeper ODM penetration and broader CSP ASIC participation.
LEO satellite: OOC capacity positions TUC for further Starlink penetration
LEO satellite build-out is emerging as a key structural growth driver for TUC, supported

by growing exposure to a leading US LEO customer and strategic Thailand capacity. The
customer has accelerated constellation deployment to 11.1k satellites in orbit by Aug-
26, up from c.9.4k when TUC began ramping its LEO business, while end-subscribers
doubled YoY to 12mn, supporting a multi-year demand runway driven by ongoing
constellation expansion and replacement cycles. TUC's current participation remains in
ground-side user terminals, where it emerged as a second source after EMC as the

primary CCL supplier, benefiting from a technology migration from M6-grade TU-883A
to higher-value M7 materials that should drive higher ASPs and gross margins.
Importantly, TUC's Thailand facility has been designated to support the program's out-
of-China (OOC) manufacturing requirements and is well positioned to supply
downstream PCB makers, creating additional share-gain opportunities as capacity
ramps. Beyond user terminals, TUC represents an attractive satellite-side option, where

incumbent CCL suppliers remain Isola and EMC; the company is undergoing
qualification for M8 materials, a step up from the M7 platform used in the prior V3-
generation satellite design. Successful qualification would expand TUC's content
opportunity into higher-value satellite applications and represents upside not yet
embedded in current expectations. We therefore view TUC's LEO growth thesis as a
combination of expanding user-terminal volumes, material upgrades from M6 to M8,

greater utilization of its Thailand OOC capacity, and potential entry into satellite
applications through M8 qualification, positioning the company to capture a growing
share of the rapidly scaling LEO ecosystem.
Strong Positioning in High-Growth Power Applications
Beyond networking and AI servers, power applications have become an increasingly
important growth driver for TUC, accounting for roughly 15% of revenue in Q326, up
from 8% in Q325. The segment, which mainly consists of AI power supply units (PSUs),

but also EV charging and consumer fast-charging applications, has been growing at
nearly 50% YoY, matching the growth rate of the company's M7/M8 business.  UBS
estimates the AI Power TAM will expand from US$18bn in 2026E to US$55bn in 2028E,
supporting demand for power-related CCL as AI infra scales (LINK).
TUC appears particularly well positioned to capture this growth. The company supplies
power-related materials to key customers including one dominant Taiwanese power

supplier that holds ~70% global market share, with Shengyi serving as the primary
second source. Management highlighted that safety and reliability remain critical
considerations, particularly as the industry moves toward higher-voltage architectures,
creating sticky customer relationships and high barriers to entry.
Thicker copper should provide a second growth lever through rising material content
and ASPs. Standard PCBs typically use 1-2oz copper layers, while PCBs with copper

thickness above 3oz (105µm) are generally classified as thick-copper PCBs. Greater
copper thickness reduces electrical resistance and heat generation at a given current,
making it particularly suitable for high-current power applications. Industry
specifications are moving from around 3oz toward 5-6oz as AI power-delivery
requirements increase. TUC has secured thick-copper CCL programs since 2025, and we
expect the combination of higher copper content and more demanding processing

requirements to increase CCL ASPs and material value per board. We view power
applications as an underappreciated growth vector that combines strong market
positioning, structural end-demand and rising content per system.

### Page 5

Key expansion focus on Mainland China and Thailand, with existing capacity in
Taiwan
TUC's manufacturing footprint started in Hsinchu, Taiwan where it started mass
production in 1997 and mass-lamination services from 2001. The company then
expanded into Mainland China, adding its Changshu plant in 2005 and Zhongshan
plant in 2010.
TUC entered 2026 with 2.4mn sheets/month of CCL capacity across Taiwan, Mainland
China and Thailand, following the ramp-up of its first 300k sheets/month in Thailand

end of 2025. A further 300k sheets/month expansion at the Thailand plant began
contributing in July 2026 and is expected to reach full production in Q426, bringing
group capacity to 2.8mn sheets/month by end-2026.
In Mar 2026, TUC announced a new round of capex investment in Changshu and
Thailand, with planned capex of Rmb953mn and Bt6.1bn, respectively. The projects will
add 600k sheets/month at each site, with Changshu expected to commence production

in H127 and Thailand adding 300k sheets/month in each of Q327 and Q427. In Jul
2026, the company announced another expansion cycle for 2028-29, investing
Rmb2.36bn in a new Zhongshan facility for an additional 1.2mn sheets/month and
Bt7.79bn in further Thailand expansion of  additional  750k sheets/month.  We expect
group capacity to reach c.5.35mn sheets/month by end-2028, 5.95mn by end-2029,
and 7.45mn by end-2030  as these projects progressively come online.

Shareholder structure:
Publicly listed on the Taipei Exchange in Dec 2003, TUC was founded by Wen-Bing Hsin
in 1974 and remains controlled by the Hsin family. Current chairman  Chung-Heng Hsin
holds ~3.8% of outstanding shares, while family investment vehicles DingXingFeng Co.
and Chang Yu Hong Co. together hold ~4.7%, bringing total family-related ownership
to ~13%. FINI ownership peaked at ~56% in 2020 amid strong 5G infrastructure

demand, before declining as US restrictions on Huawei disrupted China 5G equipment
production and slowed related CCL demand, followed by 2022-23 electronics inventory
correction. It bottomed at ~21% in 2023 and had recovered to ~39% in 2025/26 on AI/
ASIC re-rating, before easing to ~33% recently (vs Taiex's FINI holding 47%).

### Page 6

Taiwan Union Technology Corporation UBS Research
Financial analysis
Q326 earnings beat on strong GM expansion
Q326 EPS of NT$12.88 came in above consensus of NT$12.10 on stronger sales and
GM. Sales of NT$17.95bn rose 25.5% QoQ and 122.6% YoY, while GM expanded
7.2ppts QoQ to 36.9%, benefiting from price hikes, product mix improvement and the
earlier-than-expected Thailand capacity ramp, which helped the Thailand operation turn
profitable in Q3. Opex increased 19% QoQ, below the 26% QoQ revenue growth,

supporting operating leverage and OPM expansion to 31.0%; management expects the
opex ratio to decline as revenue scales. By technology, M7/M8 accounted for 38% of
sales, down 1ppt QoQ as price hikes were implemented earlier for mid-to-low end CCL
products, although management noted continued shift towards high-end CCL mix, with
M7 to grow faster than M8 in the near term. M4/M6 fell 3ppts to 28% (+ 13% QoQ),
while HDI & non-low-loss expanded 3ppts to 19% (+49% QoQ), and Others, including

Power, gained 1ppt of mix to 15% (+42% QoQ). Management highlighted particularly
strong growth in Power, a high-ASP, high-margin segment where TUC has strong
competitiveness and sees as one of its key development focuses.
Q426 to see continued pricing momentum.
We forecast Q426 sales of NT$21.24bn, up 18% QoQ, supported by another 100k
sheets of Thailand Phase I capacity following the 200k-sheet ramp in Q326, alongside
tight demand across multiple applications.  We expect GM to remain strong at 37.1% as

high-end product price hikes start to take effect in Q426E, reflecting raw-material cost
increases that TUC absorbed in prior quarters. Management expects sub-M7 product
pricing to remain firm into H127F given continued E-glass cloth tightness, while high-
end copper foil and LDK supply could tighten into 2027 and support further price
increases. We forecast Q426E EPS of NT$16.15, up 25% QoQ.

Pricing and capacity ramp drive earnings growth through 2028E
We forecast TUC’s revenue to grow 80%/51% YoY in 2027/2028E, supported by
substantial capacity expansion and broad-based demand across AI servers, networking,
optical modules, satellite and Power applications. Near-term growth should  benefit from

continued price increases, with hikes in lower-end CCL extending into 2027 and higher-
end products beginning to see price increases. Capacity additions in Changshu,
Zhongshan and Thailand support volume growth, with group capacity expected to
increase 31%/39% YoY in 2027/2028E. TUC is also building qualifications for next-
generation CSP platforms, with current efforts focused on M8/M9 opportunities,
though we expect growth to be stepwise as customer programs move through

### Page 7

qualification and ramp-up. We believe networking and optical applications should
provide additional growth, with optical modules requiring at least M7 and potentially
M9 materials, while PCIe 6 adoption supports further general server demand. We also
expect Power to become an increasingly important driver, given its strong growth,
attractive ASP/margin profile and TUC’s competitive positioning in the segment.
We expect earnings growth to outpace revenue growth through 2028E as pricing, richer

product mix and operating leverage compound the benefit from higher volumes. We
forecast GM to increase from 33.5% in 2026E to 38.7%/40.7% in 2027/2028E,
reflecting a greater contribution from M8/M9 and other high-value products, continued
pricing benefits and improving scale. At the operating level, we expect opex to grow
slower than revenue as the business scales, providing a second layer of margin
expansion, with OPM rising from 27.4% in 2026E to 33.1%/35.7% in 2027/2028E. As a

result, we forecast EPS of NT$92.06/NT$149.51 in 2027/2028E, up 121%/62% YoY
versus revenue growth of 80%/51%, respectively, highlighting the accelerating earnings
benefit from both GM expansion and operating leverage.

### Page 8

Sensitivity analysis
Quantifying earnings downside  risks if AI spending slows down from 2028
The US Department of Commerce measures announced on 16 November seek to
prevent China AI-chip controls from being circumvented through third-country data
centers. The rules extend controls beyond the physical shipment of advanced chips to
who ultimately accesses the compute, while introducing licensing requirements for
advanced-chip exports, re-exports and transfers to Singapore, Malaysia, Indonesia and

Thailand, as well as Brazil, which BIS identifies as presenting higher-than-normal
diversion risk. Chips destined for US-headquartered companies or US-parented
subsidiaries are exempt from this country-level requirement, subject to reporting
conditions. The rules do not altogether prohibit ASEAN AI investment, but raise licensing
and compliance hurdles for parts of the data center ecosystem (LINK). This raises
concerns on first-order impacts of likely to be delayed GPU deployment and slower data

center build-outs. While we believe AI data center build-out remains supported by  cloud
computing power growth, enterprise AI and inference, we note it is constrained by data-
sovereignty and latency requirements, as well as power and land constraints elsewhere.
We see market continues to expect strong 2027 but now more cautious for 2028. We
view some market expectations of a down year might be too conservative as UBS team
has been noting that operating cash flow should support sustainable capex for 2028.

That being said, we still see some sensitivity around potential acts might cap
hyperscalers on securing permitted sites and/or receiving power/water permits.
Below we examine the potential downside risks into 2028 earnings if AI spending
materially slows down vs our current base case scenario. Our key assumptions and
findings are as follows:
 If capacity expansion plan falls short of expectations...: The company has
announced plans to expand CCL monthly capacity from 2.41mn sheets in 2025 to

5.35mn/7.45mn sheets by 2028F/2030F. As we take a more conservative stance to
factor in the AI demand uncertainties, our current model assumes its CCL monthly
capacity of 4.59mn sheets in 2028E, below the company's announced plan of
5.35mn sheets;  and note that if capacity additions were only
50%/30%/20%/10% executed compared to our base case scenario, this will
imply potential 2028E earnings downside of 26%/36%/40%/45% to our current
estimates.

 ...and pricing environment becomes less favourable: We currently expect to
see AI CCL content value increases driven by computing and networking/
interconnect demands; and extended equipment lead time and qualification
process along with increasing manufacturing challenges are also suggesting more
constrained upstream inputs. That being said, if pricing power starts to weaken on
AI spending slowdown, assuming pricing falls short of our current estimates by

5%/10%/15%/20%, on top of lowered capacity, this will imply potential earnings
downside of 32%/45%/53%/60% to our current 2028E estimates.

TUC P/E (x)TUC share price (NT$)
Source: Refinitiv Eikon
TUC’s 12-month forward P/E historically traded below 15x prior to 2023, before re-

rating with the onset of the AI cycle. The re-rating accelerated from 2025 through 2026,
driven by 1) expectations for CCL price increases amid tightening supply, supporting a
stronger margin outlook; 2) growing confidence in TUC's M8/M8+ positioning and
exposure to hyperscaler ASIC programmes; and 3) capacity expansion, including in
Thailand to support higher-end demand and further product mix improvement.  These
factors lifted its forward P/E to a historical peak of 39x in June 2026. Since the AI cycle

began, TUC has traded within a roughly 10–40x forward P/E range, averaging around
17x.
Following its sharp re-rating, TUC’s share price has corrected 65% from its recent peak
since October, versus TAIEX down 28% amid broader tech-sector weakness and profit-
taking following a strong run. TUC now trades at 7x/4x our 2027E/2028E EPS, with its
12-month forward P/E at one standard deviation below its historical average. Our
earnings forecasts are below Street estimates, reflecting our view that TUC’s greater

exposure to mainstream CCL products could leave it more exposed to pricing pressure
and constrain margin expansion. Nevertheless, we expect a gradual improvement in its
product mix: while M7/M8 represented only 36% of 2025 sales, we expect TUC’s high-
end exposure to increase as networking migrates from 800G toward 1.6T,  and higher-
value power and LEO applications contribute more meaningfully. This mix shift, together
with operating leverage, underpins our forecast for gross margin to expand from 33.5%

in 2026E to 40.7% in 2028E and EPS growth to materially outpace revenue growth.
Although we take a more cautious view than the Street on the extent of this
improvement, we believe the current valuation remains attractive even on our
conservative earnings forecasts.
Our NT$1,150 price target is based on 8x 2028E P/E, toward the lower end of TUC’s
historical 7–17x average forward P/E trading range. Our conservative multiple reflects
broader tech-sector selling, rising concerns over slower AI spending and uncertainty

around PTFE adoption, alongside TUC’s relatively lower high-end CCL exposure. Our
target multiple compares with 12x/10x for EMC/ITEQ. We believe the discount to EMC
appropriately reflects TUC’s product positioning, while its improving earnings profile
supports a premium to the broader CCL and PCB groups, which trade at 8x and 7x
2028E P/E on average, respectively.
TUC  is trading at NT$623.00 (as of 20
Nov 2026)

UPSIDE (NT$1,825): We assume 2028E sales growth of 58.6% YoY, reflecting faster
M8/M9 qualification and ramp-up at next-generation CSP platforms, stronger
networking, optical and power demand, and sustained CCL pricing. We assume GM of
41.5% and EPS of NT$158.7, reflecting a richer high-end mix and stronger operating
leverage. We apply 12x 2028E P/E to derive our NT$1,825 upside valuation.
BASE (NT$1,150): We assume 2028E sales growth of 50.8% YoY, supported by

continued AI demand, M8/M9 product upgrades, growth in networking, optical and
power applications, and capacity expansion. We assume GM of 40.7%, OPM of 35.7%
and EPS of NT$149.5. We apply 8x 2028E P/E to derive our NT$1,150 base-case
valuation.
DOWNSIDE (NT$375): We assume 2028E sales growth of 21.7% YoY, reflecting slower
AI/CSP demand, delays in M8/M9 qualification and ramp-up, and earlier normalization in
CCL pricing as new E-glass supply comes online. We assume GM of 35.7% and EPS of NT

$75.1, reflecting weaker high-end mix and operating leverage. We apply 5x 2028E P/E to
derive our NT$375 downside valuation.

### Page 13

Taiwan Union Technology Corporation UBS Research
Company Description
Company Description
Taiwan Union Technology (TUC) was founded in 1974 and is a
leading global supplier of copper-clad laminate (CCL), prepreg
and mass-lamination services for printed circuit boards. The
company specializes in high-speed, low-loss materials, with
products used across telecom infrastructure, high-speed
networking, servers and data centers, automotive electronics and

LEO satellite applications. In 2025, ELL/SLL (M7/M8) accounted
for 36% of CCL revenue, followed by VLL/LL (M4/M6) at 32%,
HDI/N-LL at 24% and other products at 8%. TUC's
manufacturing footprint spans Taiwan, mainland China and
Thailand.
Industry Outlook
The CCL industry outlook remains positive, supported by AI
server investment and upgrades to high-speed networking
infrastructure. Larger, higher-layer-count PCBs and tighter signal-
integrity requirements are increasing demand for high-

performance, ultra-low-loss laminates, supporting both material
content growth and a richer product mix. Tight supply of
specialty glass fabric and other inputs is supporting laminate
price increases, although rising material costs and supply
constraints remain risks to margins and shipment growth. These
trends should favour suppliers with advanced material
capabilities and reliable access to critical inputs.

Company Description
Taiwan Union Technology (TUC) was founded in 1974 and is a leading global supplier of
copper-clad laminate (CCL), prepreg and mass-lamination services for printed circuit boards.
The company specializes in high-speed, low-loss materials, with products used across telecom

infrastructure, high-speed networking, servers and data centers, automotive electronics and
LEO satellite applications. In 2025, ELL/SLL (M7/M8) accounted for 36% of CCL revenue,
followed by VLL/LL (M4/M6) at 32%, HDI/N-LL at 24% and other products at 8%. TUC's
manufacturing footprint spans Taiwan, mainland China and Thailand.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value TUC using a PE-based methodology.
Key risks include: 1) slower-than-expected high-end CCL adoption and AI infrastructure

deployments or delays in server upgrade cycles; 2) delays in new capacity expansion and
production ramp-up; 3) stronger-than-expected competition and market-share losses in high-
end CCL and power applications; and 4) higher glass fiber, copper foil and other raw-material
costs and weaker-than-expected cost pass-through to customers.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 3

King Yuan Electronics Co
Valuation
Our target price for KYEC of NT$286 is derived using a DCF methodology. Using Bloomberg data, we apply a risk-free rate of
4%, a risk premium of 5%, and a beta of 1.4x for a 10.8% cost of equity. With 20% corporate tax and a 3.0% interest rate on its
debt, our WACC for KYEC is 9.7%. We assume terminal sales growth of 5%. We model its adjusted EBIT margin to gradually

improve from 23% in 2024 to c30% in 2035E. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027/28E P/B of 4x/4x and
2027/28E P/E of 25x/17x, in line with its global peers.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent KYEC shares from reaching our target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected end-
demand for global smartphone/consumer electronic products; 2) slower ramp for AI-related products; 3) slower-than-
expected demand recovery in the automotive and base station sector; and 4) pricing pressure on increasing competition.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

### Page 6

현대차(005380)

Q. 3Q26 법인세비용 증가 원인 및 4Q26 전망 
- 일회성 요인보다는 법인별 손익 믹스 및 적용 세율 차이에 따른 영향 
- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

## Document 228: [012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다한화에어로스페이스(012450) 3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다3Q26_ 그래도 믿는다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
 당 연구원은 한화에어로스페이스(012450)의 일부 비용을 지원받아 한화에어로스페이스 공장 투어(2026년 10월 1-2)를 다녀왔습니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

종목추천 투자등급

Source PDF: all report pdf/[012450] 한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick한화에어로스페이스(012450) 여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick_cd366e43.pdf
Report source: Hankyung

### Page 1

(012450) 
한화에어로스페이스 
2026.10.30

AI 로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

여전히 비중확대 의견, 그리고 Top-Pick 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 1,660,000 원(유지) 
 현재주가 760,000 원(10/29)

 3Q26 연결 매출액은 6조 943억원(-6.0%yoy), 영업이익은 4,520억원(-
46.2%yoy, OPM 7.4%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센 1조 1,614억원을 하회.  
 지상방산의 손익이 크게 악화. 영업이익은 217억원(OPM 1.7%) 기록. 주요 
납품이 4분기에 집중된 가운데 일회성 비용 약 500억원(대전사업장 복구·
정상화 약 300억원, 국내 사업 충당금 약 160억원)과 환율 영향이 겹침.   
 다만 내용을 살펴보면 수출 사업의 수익성 훼손은 크지 않았다는 판단임.  
 일회성 제외 영업이익은 약 717억원(OPM 5.8%)이며, 일회성 비용은 대전
사업장이 국내 물량을 전담한다는 점에서 전액 국내 귀속으로 추정. 내수 
OPM을 일회성 제외 -5% 내외로 가정하면, 내수 영업이익은 약 -826억원
(일회성 포함 OPM -12.7%), 수출은 1,043억원(OPM 17.6%)으로 추정됨. 
 1Q26 대비로는 국내 마진 하락이, 2Q26 대비로는 수출 물량 감소가 가장 
크게 작용(도표 참고). 국내는 저마진 사업 매출이 늘고 고마진 반복 양산 물
량이 줄며 일시적으로 믹스가 악화. 수출은 폴란드 등 주요 인도가 정체되어 
매출이 감소한 가운데 저수익 사업 중심으로 믹스가 악화된 영향. 
 4Q26부터 회복 구간 진입 전망. 폴란드 K9 2차 실행계약 물량의 현지 인
도가 본격화되며, 회사는 2026년 폴란드 K9 30문 이상, 천무 발사대 60대 
이상(*연초 40대에서 상향) 인도를 계획. 수출입 데이터상으로도 폴란드·루
마니아향 출하가 대규모로 진행 중이며 폴란드향 부속류 수출도 정상적으로 
이어지는 것으로 확인. 일회성 소멸과 고마진 수출 집중으로 수출 OPM은 
30% 내외로 회복할 것. 다만 국내는 정상 가동(2027년 상반기 목표) 전까
지 한 자릿수 초반 마진에 머물 것으로 전망. 
 중장기 수익성 우려도 크지 않다는 판단. 2027~2029년 매출 연 15% 성
장 가이던스는 유지됐고, 내년 폴란드 K9·천무 인도 물량은 모두 올해 대비 
증가할 계획. 폴란드 사업 이후 높아진 수출 마진 레벨은 후속 사업에서도 
유지될 전망. 수주는 중동 지정학 불안으로 지연되고 있으나 대공체계 등 문
의가 이어지고 있어, 2027년부터 중동이 수주의 중심이 될 것으로 기대. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 166만원을 유지함. 3Q26 지상방산 
쇼크는 국내 적자와 수출 물량 공백이 겹친 일시적 결과로, 수출 경쟁력 훼
손으로 보기 어려움. 실적 충격에 따른 주가 조정은 분할 매수 기회로 판단.  
 한화에어로스페이스의 주가는 4월 7일 사상 최고가 153.7만원에서 10월 
29일 76.0만원으로 -51% 조정 받은 상황. 12MF P/E 기준 14.3x, 27E 
P/E는 13.9x로 밸류에이션 관점에서도 매력적인 구간 진입. 그동안 3분기 
실적 우려 등으로 부진했다면, 이제는 실적 바텀아웃과 대형 수주 랠리 속 
다시 한 번 반등을 기록할 차례. 동사를 방산업종 Top-pick으로 유지함.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율 
종목추천 및 업종추천 투자기간: 12 개월  (추천기준일 종가대비 추천종목의 예상 목표수익률을 의미함) 당사 투자의견 비율(%) 
ㆍSTRONG BUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +50%이상 3% 
ㆍBUY(매수) 추천기준일 종가대비 +15%이상 ~ +50%미만 94% 
ㆍHOLD(중립) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%이상 ∼ +15%미만 3% 
ㆍREDUCE(매도) 추천기준일 종가대비 -10%미만 0%

## Document 234: [034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수 주 확대 본격화두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차_47af979a

Source PDF: all report pdf/[034020] 두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차. 2026 SMR 첫걸음. 2H27 원전 수 주 확대 본격화두산에너빌리티(034020) 시장과 산업의 시차_47af979a.pdf

(26.10.29)

유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

표 2. 웨스팅하우스의 미국 및 글로벌 신규 원전 프로젝트 현황 (초기 사업발굴 단계 제외)  
 프로젝트·사업 기수 발전용량 Cameco 분류 단계 및 비고 
미국 DOE 공급망 금융지원 사업 최대 10기 최대 11.5GW 장납기 품목 조달 금융지원, 7개 유틸리티와 논의 중 
 미 상무부 전략적 협력사업 최대 10기 최대 11.5GW 정부 협력사업(한국은 6기 투자/건설)

표 3. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 
항목 공식 발표 내용 
조건부 대출 약정 규모 최대 175억 달러·US$17.5 billion 
지원 대상 최대 5개 프로젝트, 프로젝트당 AP1000 2기, 합계 10기 
프로젝트 소유 구조 Westinghouse와 해당 전력회사 또는 에너지기업이 공동 소유 
대출 접근 전 자기자본 요건 프로젝트당 Westinghouse 5억 달러, 상대 전력회사·에너지기업 5억 달러, 합계 10억 달러를 사전에 확약 
조달 방식 Westinghouse가 참여 기업과 함께 장납기품목을 고정가격으로 조달 
조달 시점 자기자본 확약 시점과 기타 조건에 따라 프로젝트별 구매를 순차적으로 진행

DAISHIN SECURITIES

표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

## Document 274: Are we close to a bottom_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Are we close to a bottom_.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Are we close to a bottom?  
Summary 
Event: Markets corrected sharply on Friday, with the S&P 
500 down 3.7% and the Nasdaq 100 down 5.7%, while the 
VIX surged above 35. Most sectors ended lower, although 
defensives such as healthcare and consumer staples fared 
better. At the industry level, semiconductors once again led 
the losses, falling more than 10%. Gold also declined sharply 
as systematic selling added to the pressure, while bonds 
gained in flight to safety.  
Our View: While we acknowledge company-specific risks 
and downside risk to consensus AI capex estimates, much of 
this risk now appears to be priced in. We, therefore, 
recommend investors avoid overreacting and stay invested, 
while using elevated volatility through structured strategies 
and selectively building exposure to quality assets, including 
our latest opportunistic idea, US mega caps. A diversified 
multi-asset portfolio, including bonds, would serve as a 
strong foundation to weather market volatilities. 
What is driving the sell-off? 
With most valuation and sentiment metrics flashing oversold 
conditions, it appears that the market has moved beyond the 
traditional investment framework, with panic now driving the 
selloff as the typical policy support, or “Trump put,” remains 
absent so far. During previous sharp corrections, such as the 
tariff-driven sell-off in 2025, or the pandemic shock in March 
2020, a change in policy provided support to markets and 
helped trigger a sharp rebound. At this stage, not only is 
policy support missing, but headlines such as a potential SEC 
investigation into Nvidia are adding uncertainty.  
The ongoing correction remains largely centred on the AI 
theme. We have been arguing that there is 10-15% downside 
risk to consensus AI capex estimates, or roughly USD 200bn 
lower per annum. However, the roughly USD 3trn erosion in 
global semiconductor market capitalisation this month alone 
suggests the market is already discounting a more severe 
outcome. 
 US equity markets corrected sharply on Friday, 20-Nov

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 23 November 2026

What can calm investor concerns?   
We believe three factors could help: clearer visibility 
around interest rates, greater clarity on neocloud AI 
capex trends as several industry conferences take place 
over the next few weeks, and improved visibility around 
Nvidia's leadership given its critical role in the AI industry. 
Traditional support signals, such as the VIX reaching 25-30 
or market drawdowns exceeding 10%, have failed to deliver 
this time. The Nasdaq 100 is already around 20% below its 
peak, while the S&P 500 is down over 10%. While it is difficult 
to call a bottom, the S&P 500 has historically delivered strong 
positive returns on average after a 10% drawdown, over the 
next 6-12 months.  
Stay invested as structural growth is on sale 
Last month, we downgraded global equities to Neutral as 
markets transitioned into a mid-cycle environment. Even by 
our conservative standards, however, the current correction 
looks excessive. We recommend investors avoid 
overreacting and stay invested, as the fundamental backdrop 
remains supportive.  
At current levels, we estimate the market is effectively 
discounting around USD 500bn per annum of cuts to 
consensus AI capex estimates, considerably more than our 
roughly USD 200bn downside scenario. Such an outcome 
would effectively imply that most remaining AI capex is 
directed toward inference, with only limited spending on 
training. In our view, this is at odds with how the AI industry 
is progressing, particularly given the strong training pipeline 
and intensifying race toward Artificial General Intelligence. 
At the same time, AI adoption continues to expand across 
both enterprises and consumers, with US AI adoption 
reaching around 25% based on the latest October data. We 
estimate this growth could require an additional 15-20GW of 
inference capacity, equivalent to roughly USD 700-900bn per 
annum of investment. Against this backdrop, the downside 
risks to consensus capex estimates appear manageable. 
Greater clarity around these numbers at upcoming industry 
conferences should help ease investor concerns. 
While we expect sanity to eventually prevail and investors to 
stop indiscriminately selling good business models, we 
continue to advocate diversification. During the Q1 and Q3 
2026 sell-offs, we pounded the table on semiconductors and 
introduced an opportunistic idea on the industry. This time, 
while acknowledging that value has clearly emerged, we 
prefer adding exposure through more diversified 
opportunities such as US IT and our recently-introduced US 
mega-cap ideas. We still expect a moderate reduction in 
consensus AI capex estimates, which argues for remaining 
selective within semiconductors. Investors in US equities 
should also be diversified across sectors (table on right). We 
 Since 2010, there have been nine drawdowns of 10% or 
more in the S&P 500. Average returns over the next 6-12 
months have been 9-15%, with negative returns in one 
instance, 2022

see four key sector blocks in our framework for the S&P 500. 
We are overweight on US information technology (IT), US 
Financials, US Materials and US Healthcare, drawn from 
each of the four key sector blocks. 
Gold – Still waiting for a bottom. Gold depreciated sharply 
to just under USD 3,600/oz last week, likely reflecting forced 
liquidation and margin calls as equity losses deepened. With 
our 3-month price target at USD 4,000/oz, we will be closely 
tracking market technical levels and investor positioning to 
get a sense when we reach a near-term bottom. Fractal 
signals seem to indicate the risk of a reversal may be 
looming. We will issue another Market Watch should these 
indicators emerge.  
FX – USD nearing a peak. The USD looks like it may be near 
a peak in this current cycle on peak Fed hawkishness and we 
retain our 12-month target of 98.5 on the DXY, which also 
supports our constructive gold price target of USD 4,500/oz 
in 12 months.  
Bonds – Providing better diversification benefits. US 
government bonds have resumed their negative correlation 
to risk assets and we continue to recommend an allocation to 
the 3-to-7-year tenor for high-quality corporate credit. Any 
rebound in risk sentiment could see the 10-year US 
government bond yield return to our 3-month target range of 
5.00 to 5.25%, which we believe would be attractive for 
investors.  
— Sundeep Gantori, CFA, CAIA CIO, Equities 
— Jonathan Liang, CFA, CIO, Fixed Income & FX

### Page 2

Weekly Economics
U.S. Week Ahead
Existing Home Sales • Thursday
Housing market conditions remain highly unfavorable. Mortgage
rates are the primary headwind, and they have climbed even higher
alongside the rise in longer-term yields. At the same time, low
supply is preventing a broad correction in home prices, leaving
prospective buyers squeezed on two fronts.
We expect that existing home sales slid 3.4% in August to an
annualized rate of 3.92 million. Behind this forecast is ongoing

weakness in mortgage applications and a recent bounce in
mortgage rates, which rose from 6.43% at the beginning of July to
6.66% by month-end.
We do not expect affordability conditions to meaningfully improve
in the near term. Supply is likely to remain constrained while high
mortgage rates discourage mobility. And mortgage rates are apt
to remain elevated amid firm economic growth expectations and
heavy government and corporate debt issuance.
(Return)

### Page 3

Economics Week Ahead Economics
CPI • Friday
Headline inflation looks to have picked up last month. We estimate
the CPI rose 0.40% in August, with gasoline prices increasing a little
over 4% as renewed tensions in the Middle East pushed oil higher.
Grocery prices also likely rebounded after modestly declining in July.
Outside of food and energy, price pressures appear little changed.
We expect the core CPI to rise 0.23%, essentially matching July's

pace. Core goods inflation should remain firm, led by another solid
increase in used vehicle prices as consumer prices continue to
catch up to the trend indicated at wholesale auctions. Meantime,
household furnishings and other tariff-sensitive categories appear
to be stabilizing after declining earlier this year.
Core services inflation should be little changed with another 0.2%
monthly increase. But underneath the surface, we expect a modest
pickup in travel (led by lodging away from home) to offset slightly

cooler medical care and housing cost growth.
All told, we expect the report to show that while headline inflation
remains influenced by the ebbs and flows of the conflict in the
Middle East, core inflation remains contained. Should our forecast
prove correct, core CPI will ease to 2.4% year-over-year, in line with
its 6-month annualized rate but above the more recent 3-month
annualized pace of 1.7%.

### Page 4

Weekly Economics
G10 Week Ahead
European Central Bank Monetary Policy Meeting •
Thursday
We expect the European Central Bank (ECB) to raise the Deposit
Rate by 25 bps to 2.50% next week. August headline inflation
remained elevated at 3.3% year-over-year, though softer core
and services inflation suggest underlying pressures continue to
moderate. Still, the more hawkish tone of the July minutes and
renewed rise in energy prices should keep policymakers cautious.

The recent escalation in the Middle East has pushed fuel prices
at the pump close to their 2022 peaks, while natural gas storage
remains below historical levels, leaving the Eurozone vulnerable
to further energy price increases. The ECB is unlikely to respond
aggressively if the shock remains confined to energy given the
downside risks to growth. However, a broader rise in food inflation
linked to El Niño would be more concerning, as highly visible prices

could lift inflation expectations and generate second-round effects.
For now, resilient Q3 activity, elevated headline inflation and
contained wage growth support one final hike. The ECB will likely
retain and emphasize its scenario-based outlook in the updated
staff projections given continued uncertainty around the conflict
and energy prices. The policy path beyond September will depend
on whether energy and food pressures persist and begin to spread,

which could leave room for further tightening.
(Return)

U.K. Monthly GDP • Friday
We expect UK GDP to rise 0.2% month-over-month in July, with
growth of 0.3% on a three-month-over-three-month basis.

Activity likely moderated after June’s strong rebound, though
services remained supportive as the July services PMI rose to 52.1.
Retail sales volumes fell 0.5% month-over-month, while softer
manufacturing activity likely reflected some reversal of earlier
precautionary stockbuilding.
The moderation appears more consistent with payback from
June than a broader deterioration in momentum. August PMIs
remain consistent with Q3 GDP growth of around 0.3% quarter-

over-quarter. Still, the renewed escalation in the Middle East and
associated rise in gas and energy prices adds upside risk to inflation
and could weigh on household demand. Against this backdrop and
with Bank of England hawks continuing to emphasize the need for
tighter policy, we expect the BoE to remain cautious and continue
to look for a rate hike in Q4. -1.0%

### Page 5

Economics Week Ahead Economics
EM Week Ahead
Mexico CPI • Wednesday
We expect Mexico’s August inflation rate to edge higher, with
headline CPI rising to 3.34% year-over-year (0.27% month-over-
month) from 3.26% year-over-year in early August. Part of the
pickup likely reflects less favorable base effects and a smaller
disinflationary contribution from food prices. While agricultural
prices continued to help restrain inflation in mid-August, the drag

was notably less pronounced than in July. Core inflation should
remain elevated at 3.98% year-over-year (0.25% month-over-
month).
While inflation remains within Banxico’s midpoint 3% target,
persistent core and services inflation suggest convergence toward
the midpoint of the target could prove gradual. For now, we
continue to expect Banxico to keep rates on hold, with the policy
rate remaining at 6.50% through the rest of 2026 and into early
2027 as policymakers await clearer evidence that underlying

inflation pressures are easing sustainably. While rate cuts remain
a possibility in 2027, the current inflation backdrop argues for
patience in the near term.

Brazil CPI • Friday
We expect Brazil’s August inflation rate to slow to 4.20% year-over-
year, helped by lower electricity, food and fuel prices. The main
driver should be a temporary reduction in residential electricity bills
stemming from a one-off credit, while recent data already point to
a notable easing in price pressures. Mid-August CPI came in weaker
than expected, with inflation falling to 4.24% year-over-year amid
declines in electricity, food, fuel and airfare prices. Beyond these

temporary factors, restrictive monetary policy continues to weigh
on economic activity, as evidenced by the recent slowdown in Q2
GDP growth. However, underlying inflation risks remain. A still-
tight labor market and government measures aimed at supporting
consumption could keep services inflation sticky, making progress
toward the 3.0% inflation target more gradual.
Overall, the inflation outlook remains consistent with additional
policy easing. We expect COPOM to deliver a 25 bps rate cut in

September, bringing the Selic Rate to 13.75%. Beyond September,
policymakers are likely to proceed cautiously as they assess
incoming inflation data and the impact of the election cycle on
inflation expectations. Our base case remains for a pause after
September, with additional easing more likely in 2027.

## Document 324: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3 according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever-volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2-0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues. As anticipated by our Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2). 
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%). According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2), driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export growth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports (-1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q). Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, growth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026, 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate (AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheless 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI-related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

利成長的主要驅動力。高盛指出，第三季AI

基礎建設相關企業將貢獻標普500獲利增長
的54%。此外，大型CSP之雲端業務營收有
望加速成長，反映AI運算及雲端服務需求持
續提升，有助支撐後續資本支出擴張動能。
► 近期市場波動仍受高利率環境影響，壓抑投
資人風險偏好與估值擴張空間，顯示市場目
前壓力更多來自評價面而非基本面。隨財報
數據陸續公布，若企業持續繳出優於預期的
獲利表現，並維持正向財測，市場有望逐步
回歸基本面評價，使企業獲利成長逐步支撐
股市表現。
美企財報獲利穩健，對股市中長期有撐
熱門議題
美股第三季獲利成長持續上修，科技為主要支撐 CSP雲端營收加速，資本支出變現跡象持續受驗證
評價面疑慮下，股價難以進一步擴張

投資焦點
► 醫療保健、醫療服務與生物技術等行業，在
景氣不好時，市場預期民眾對醫療保健產業
的需求 不易 下降 ， 成為傳 統防禦 型類 股代
表。目前金融環境利率偏高，11月美國總統
大選國會恐變天，股市波動風險上升，觀察
過去當VIX恐慌指數上揚時，醫療保健產業
相較於科技股更穩健，具有更好抗震能力。
► 醫療與生技類股近一年漲幅明顯，主因1)生
技股過去一段時間受到高利率、融資成本及
臨床試驗風險壓抑，部分公司估值偏低。當
臨床數據改善、或導入AI篩選資料與藥物，
投資人重新評價未來現金流與價值。2)大型
藥廠面臨既有藥品專利到期壓力，透過收購
取得新藥與研發管線，提高有潛力的生技公
司併購價值，帶動整體產業估值回升。3)肥
胖、癌症與慢性病用藥創造長期市場需求，
創新藥物(如瘦瘦針)讓部分醫療公司成長不
再只依賴人口老化，高盛今年7月將全球減肥
藥市場2030年銷售額預估上調15%至1,140
億美元，看好此類藥品的長期商業潛力。
醫療生技近年表現佳，估今年3Q醫院客流量年增4%VIX恐慌指數上升時，健護醫療表現相較穩健

市場波動上升、醫療保健表現佳，獲利穩定成長

### Page 8

► 美國醫療費用深受法令影響，由於商業雇主
贊助保險(ESI)和平價醫療法案(ACA)的自付
額增加，以及最高自付費用要求，較去年同
期提高，使今年第三季美國整體住院人數、
手術量和醫院客流量均呈現改善。高盛預估
今年第三季醫院客流量年增4%，加上季節性
因素，下半年醫療保健利用率將較上半年成
長，整體第四季預估獲利穩健，甚至可延至
明年，反應在近期XLV ETF股價創新高。
► 展望2027年，各醫療保險計畫普遍優先考慮
透過提高會員保費，和要求費用分攤(尤其是
提高會員自付費用上限)來擴大利潤空間，並
持續優化覆蓋範圍和產品組合，預估商業雇
主提供的醫療保險(ESI)保費趨勢，將在明年
加速成長至過去20 年來的最高水準。 以
Refinitiv預估明年美國各產業獲利成長來看，
醫療保健和生技相較於今年獲利明顯增加，
反而今年獲利極佳的能源和通訊受高基期影
響，獲利增速下滑。投資人在整體金融環境
波動放大時期，宜以醫療生技股穩中求利。

股市
 美股第三季獲利年增率預估為雙位數成長，市場靜待財報結果與展望，和對AI資本支出回收影響。
考量地緣衝突、殖利率偏高、評價面壓力與鄰近美國期中選舉，短期金融市場紛擾不止，可增加防
禦股增強耐震度。長期AI、科技發展趨勢不變，逢股市拉回時可分批布局，分散配置於不同國家與
產業，降低市場整體波動，亦可以槓鈴策略(防禦+成長)穩中求利。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點，全球利率走揚有利銀行股。此外，地緣衝突頻傳，歐洲國防軍工仍
有投資題材。
策略風格：可增加防禦股配置，中長期看
好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分關注
醫療保健生技產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、歐洲國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，預估今年底前主要國家仍有升息空間，殖利率偏高環境使短中期債券提供吸
引力，逢利率走高、鎖利領息。高信評債體質較佳，具穩定現金流與還款能力，聚焦風險調整後利
差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、科技、通訊及公用事業等

匯市
 歐洲政治風險上升、歐元短期弱盤，美元高檔有撐。
 日本銀行再度升息時間不定，日圓區間震盪。
美元高檔有撐
商品
 中東地緣衝突短期難解，油價維持高位但震盪加劇。
 短期美國雙率走高，使金價短期承壓，惟美歐日財政赤字壓力與去美元化，使全球央行與長期投資
人買盤依舊，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
